# Images I-FC: canvas core, tools, keymap and workspace store. Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

> **Where and when this runs.** Unit **I-FC**, worktree `.claude/worktrees/i-fc`, branch `task/i-fc`.
> **Cut after** I-C0, M-C0 and C-C0 are all on `main` (rulings R3). Batch 2
> (`BA ∥ BK ∥ BX ∥ BS ∥ FC ∥ FB`). **Merge position:** fifth inside I's batch 2 (I order: C0; BA, BX,
> BK, BS, **FC**, FB; BP, BT, FA; FW; E). FC is on the **critical path** (C0 → FC → FA → FW → E).
> FC is built against the regenerated client and a fake API client (`@/test/fixtures` `fakeClient`,
> and the in-memory API of the canvas lab, Task 11). It needs no backend unit merged: BA's
> shape routes and BK's `ImageDetail` may still be 501 stubs on `main` when FC merges.
> Contract names follow I-C0's plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-images-c0.md`): its ruling 1
> (create and update answer a flat `BoxWriteResult` = `Box` + `repaired` + `finding_id`; `Box` itself
> carries no finding link), `ImageCamera`/`ImageDetail`, `BoxShape`, `ImageMeasurement` (with
> `label: string`, nullable `length_mm`/`sigma_mm`, ≤ 500 per image) and the delete path parameter
> `imageMeasurementId`. **Task 1 Step 1 still reads what C0 merged and corrects any drifted name
> before code is written.** The contract wins over this plan every time; FC never edits `contract/`.

**Goal:** Build the Images workspace's centre pane engine: a Konva `ImageCanvas` with four layers,
a two-level image (preview → full, `createImageBitmap`, an LRU that `close()`s), polygon LOD, the
tools V H B R P M L Del T with undo, the length-measurement maths (`measure.ts`), the images keymap
table, and the `store/imagesWorkspace.ts` store that FA and FW build on.

**Architecture:**
- `frontend/src/store/imagesWorkspace.ts` (zustand) is the single state of the open image: the
  `ImageDetail`, its shapes (`Box` rows of every `shape`), its length measurements, selection,
  tool, draft, view, toggles, the suggestion threshold, the per-image `History`, the save state and
  the canvas dialogs. Every component reads it through selectors; every handler reads it through
  `getState()` at trigger time (never a closed-over value).
- `frontend/src/images/canvas/` holds rendering and persistence: `geometry.ts` and `history.ts`
  (moved from `editor/`, shims left behind), `commands.ts` (the generalised undoable commands),
  `actions.ts` (flows that may confirm: delete, retype), `lod.ts`, `imageCache.ts`,
  `useTwoLevelImage.ts`, `ShapeLayer.tsx`, `InteractionLayer.tsx`, `ImageCanvas.tsx`,
  `useImageData.ts`, `CanvasDialogs.tsx`, and the dev-only canvas lab.
- `frontend/src/images/tools/` holds the tool registry and one file per tool (pure state machine +
  its `ToolDefinition`), `measure.ts` (the maths FW's `MeasuredSize` renders), the palette, the
  zoom cluster and the T picker.
- `frontend/src/images/workspace/keymap.ts` annotates F's `images` keymap entries with a `when`
  and dispatches them through F's `useToolShortcuts`; FA adds rows to it in batch 3.

**Tech Stack:** React 18, TypeScript 5.9, zustand 5, Konva 9 + react-konva 18, Tailwind 3.4 with
the Aero glass tokens, vitest 3 + Testing Library + jsdom. No new dependency.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-image-inspection-design.md` — I-D8, I-D9, I-D11, §5
(editor rows), §6.2 (canvas, palette, zoom cluster), §9.1, §9.2, §9.3 (canvas side and
`measure.ts`), §12 (motion on the canvas), §13, §15 (canvas budget), §17 (frontend: keymap, tool
state machines, LOD, `measure.ts`). Umbrella `2026-09-26-inspection-platform-design.md`.
Binding rulings: `.superpowers/sdd/imc-common/programme-rulings.md` (R1–R10) and the I controller
rulings in the dispatch context (file ownership rule 5, keymap rule 7, new-primitive rule 6).
Foundation choices: `docs/evidence/foundation/rulings.md`.

**Budget (AGENTS.md item 6):**
- **Background jobs:** none. FC starts no long work; every FC call is bounded by one image.
- **Synchronous, bounded by one image:** shape create/update/delete/review and measurement
  create/delete, one request each (a multi-selection delete issues ≤ selection-size requests,
  queued behind each other in the image's `History.run`); `GET …/images/{id}` (`ImageDetail`),
  `GET …/boxes` (≤ 5,000 rows, BA's cap), `GET …/measurements` (≤ 500, C0's cap). The delete
  confirmation reads `GET /findings/{id}` only for selected shapes with a known finding link.
- **Bounded reads / memory (§15 "Canvas"):** one full frame (≤ 4,000 px, `max_side` omitted) and
  ≤ 4 previews at `max_side=2048`, decoded with `createImageBitmap` into `BitmapCache`
  (`fulls: 1, previews: 4`); `close()` runs on eviction and on `clear()`; the displayed bitmap is
  pinned so it is never closed while drawn. Neighbour prefetch is ≤ 2 previews (the ids FW passes),
  after the current preview decoded. Polygon LOD memo is a `WeakMap` keyed by the `points` array, so
  it is released with the shape. No image set is ever held.
- **Frame budget:** four Konva layers; layers 2 and 3 stop listening (no hit-graph rebuild) during
  pan/zoom and for 120 ms after the last wheel or drag event; `perfectDrawEnabled(false)`,
  `shadowForStrokeEnabled(false)`, `strokeScaleEnabled={false}`; pan changes only the `Stage`
  props, never re-renders the shape nodes (`ShapeNode` is memoised on its box, selection, hover and
  LOD bucket). No animation loop on the canvas (A6).

**Execution DAG (inside the unit).**

```
T1 preflight + move ─┬─ T2 store ─┬─ T3 api, commands, actions ─┬─ T8 keymap + canvas keys ──────────────┐
                     │            └─ T6 tool registry, machines ─┼─ T9 palette, zoom, T picker, dialogs ──┤
                     ├─ T4 lod + measure (pure) ─────────────────┴─ T7 shape + interaction layers ────────┼─ T10 ImageCanvas + useImageData ─ T11 lab + gate
                     └─ T5 bitmap cache + two-level image ────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

Dependencies: T4 and T5 need only T1; T3 and T6 need T2; T7 needs T3 + T4 + T6; T8 needs T3 + T6;
T9 needs T3 + T6; T10 needs T5 + T7 + T9; T11 needs everything. Parallel batches (implementers in
this one worktree never share a file; each batch's file lists are disjoint): **B1** T1 · **B2** T2 ∥
T4 ∥ T5 · **B3** T3 ∥ T6 · **B4** T7 ∥ T8 ∥ T9 · **B5** T10 · **B6** T11. **Critical path:**
T1 → T2 → T6 → T7 → T10 → T11. Tasks are numbered in a valid sequential order, so one implementer
may simply run T1…T11.

**Interfaces provided (other units import these exact names):**

```ts
// frontend/src/store/imagesWorkspace.ts  (FA, FW, E)
export type ToolId = "select" | "pan" | "box" | "rbox" | "polygon" | "point" | "length" | (string & {});
export type Draft =
  | { kind: "rect"; anchor: Point; start: Point; rect: Rect | null }
  | { kind: "rbox"; stage: "edge" | "width"; a: Point; b: Point; c: Point | null }
  | { kind: "polygon"; points: Point[]; cursor: Point | null; pressed: boolean; lastScreen: Point | null }
  | { kind: "length"; a: Point; b: Point | null }
  | { kind: "custom"; tool: string; data: unknown };            // FA's S tool keeps its prompts here
export type ConfirmState =
  | { kind: "delete"; ids: string[]; findings: FindingDetail[] }
  | { kind: "retype"; ids: string[]; typeId: string }
  | null;
export interface ImagesWorkspaceState {
  projectId: string | null; imageId: string | null; image: ImageDetail | null;
  types: ClassDef[];
  boxes: Record<string, Box>; order: string[];
  measurements: Record<string, ImageMeasurement>;
  selectedIds: string[]; selectedMeasurementId: string | null; hoveredId: string | null;
  focusedSuggestionId: string | null;                           // FA's Tab/A/X target
  tool: ToolId; activeTypeId: string | null; draft: Draft | null;
  view: ViewTransform; viewport: Size; fitted: boolean; keepZoom: boolean; interacting: boolean;
  spaceHeld: boolean; shiftHeld: boolean; altHeld: boolean;
  showAnnotations: boolean; showSuggestions: boolean; threshold: number;
  history: History; pending: number;
  failure: { message: string; retry: (() => void) | null } | null;
  picker: { at: Point; purpose: "retype" | "active" } | null;  // `at` in canvas-container px
  confirm: ConfirmState;
  findingOf: Record<string, string>;                            // box id → finding id (FC-R16)
  // actions
  loadImage(image: ImageDetail, boxes: Box[], measurements: ImageMeasurement[]): void;
  setImage(image: ImageDetail): void;
  setTypes(types: readonly ClassDef[]): void;
  setBoxes(boxes: Box[]): void; upsertBox(box: Box): void; removeBox(id: string): void;
  patchStates(ids: string[], state: ReviewState): void;
  upsertMeasurement(m: ImageMeasurement): void; removeMeasurement(id: string): void;
  select(ids: string[], mode?: "replace" | "toggle"): void;
  selectMeasurement(id: string | null): void;
  hover(id: string | null): void; focusSuggestion(id: string | null): void;
  setTool(tool: ToolId): void; setActiveType(id: string | null): void; setDraft(d: Draft | null): void;
  setViewport(size: Size): void; setView(view: ViewTransform): void;
  fit(): void; oneToOne(): void; zoomAt(display: Point, factor: number): void; zoomBy(factor: number): void;
  centreOn(point: Point, opts?: { radiusPx?: number; animate?: boolean }): void;
  panIntoView(rect: Rect, opts?: { animate?: boolean }): void;
  setKeepZoom(on: boolean): void; setInteracting(on: boolean): void;
  setHeld(keys: Partial<{ space: boolean; shift: boolean; alt: boolean }>): void;
  toggleAnnotations(): void; toggleSuggestions(): void; setThreshold(v: number): void;
  beginRequest(): void; endRequest(): void;
  fail(message: string, retry?: () => void): void; clearFailure(): void;
  openPicker(at: Point, purpose: "retype" | "active"): void; closePicker(): void;
  setConfirm(c: ConfirmState): void;
  linkFindings(links: Record<string, string>): void;            // FW (from GET /findings?image_id=) and FA (accepts)
  unlinkFinding(boxId: string): void;
  reset(): void;
}
export const useImagesWorkspace: UseBoundStore<StoreApi<ImagesWorkspaceState>>;
export type ImagesWorkspaceStore = typeof useImagesWorkspace;
export const THRESHOLD_STEP = 0.05;
export function isAccepted(b: Box): boolean;                    // accepted | edited
export function acceptedShapes(s): Box[];                       // layer 2 ([] when annotations hidden)
export function pendingSuggestions(s): Box[];                   // unreviewed ≥ threshold, confidence ↓ ([] when hidden)
export function selectedShapes(s): Box[];
export function singleSelected(s): Box | null;
export function typeOf(s, typeId: string): ClassDef | undefined;
export function saveState(s): "saved" | "saving" | "failed";
export function viewportImageRect(s): Rect;                     // visible image px, clipped (FA's SAM crop)
export function isDrawing(s): boolean;
// frontend/src/images/canvas/lod.ts  (FA's suggestion nodes pass the bucket to ShapeNode)
export function bucketOf(scale: number): number;                // round(log√2 scale)
export function lodPoints(points: number[][], bucket: number): number[];  // flat, memoised
export function waitForIdle(store?: ImagesWorkspaceStore, timeoutMs?: number): Promise<void>;

// frontend/src/images/canvas/ImageCanvas.tsx  (FW mounts it; FA and FW fill the slots)
export interface ImageCanvasProps {
  projectId: string;
  types: readonly ClassDef[];                                   // project classes (ProjectOut.classes)
  imageUrl: (imageId: string, maxSide: number | null) => string;// null = the full stored frame
  neighbourIds?: readonly string[];                             // ≤ 2 previews prefetched
  suggestions?: ReactNode;                                      // Konva nodes inside layer 3 (FA)
  overlay?: ReactNode;                                          // Konva nodes appended to layer 4 (FW marker, FA SAM preview)
  children?: ReactNode;                                         // HTML floated over the canvas (palette, zoom, chips, bars)
  onShapeCreated?: (result: BoxWriteResult) => void;           // FW opens the inspector on result.finding_id
  className?: string;
}
export interface ImageCanvasHandle {
  centreOn(point: Point, opts?: { radiusPx?: number; animate?: boolean }): void; // FW arrival (§6.5)
  panIntoView(rect: Rect, opts?: { animate?: boolean }): void;                   // FW "Show on image", row select
  fit(): void;
  stage(): Konva.Stage | null;                                                   // E's perf harness
}
export const ImageCanvas: ForwardRefExoticComponent<ImageCanvasProps & RefAttributes<ImageCanvasHandle>>;
export const LAYER_NAMES = { image: "image", annotations: "annotations", suggestions: "suggestions", interaction: "interaction" } as const;

// frontend/src/images/canvas/ShapeLayer.tsx  (FA draws suggestions with the same node)
export interface ShapeNodeProps { box: Box; colour: string; variant: "accepted" | "suggestion";
  selected: boolean; hovered: boolean; bucket: number; scale: number; interactive: boolean;
  onPointerDown?: (id: string, e: KonvaEventObject<MouseEvent>) => void;
  onDragEnd?: (id: string, e: KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void; }
export const ShapeNode: MemoExoticComponent<(p: ShapeNodeProps) => JSX.Element>;

// frontend/src/images/canvas/useImageData.ts  (FW)
export function useImageData(projectId: string, imageId: string): { loading: boolean; error: string | null };

// frontend/src/images/canvas/commands.ts  (FA: cmdReview; FW: undo/redo, severity is F's)
export interface CommandContext { api: ApiClient; projectId: string; store: ImagesWorkspaceStore; history: History }
export function useCommandContext(projectId: string): CommandContext;
export function enqueue<T>(ctx, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
export function tracked<T>(ctx, label: string, fn: () => Promise<T>, retry?: () => void): Promise<T | undefined>;
export function cmdCreateShape(ctx, imageId: string, body: BoxCreate): Promise<BoxWriteResult | undefined>;
export function cmdUpdateShape(ctx, id: string, after: ShapePatch): Promise<void>;
export function cmdSetType(ctx, ids: string[], typeId: string, opts?: { confirmFindingDelete?: boolean }): Promise<"done" | "needs-confirm" | "failed">;
export function cmdReview(ctx, ids: string[], action: "accept" | "reject"): Promise<BoxReviewResult | undefined>;

export function cmdCreateMeasurement(ctx, imageId: string, a: Point, b: Point): Promise<ImageMeasurement | undefined>;
export function cmdDeleteMeasurement(ctx, id: string): Promise<void>;
export function cmdUndo(ctx): Promise<void>; export function cmdRedo(ctx): Promise<void>;
export type ShapePatch = { kind: "rect"; rect: OrientedRect } | { kind: "points"; points: Point[] } | { kind: "point"; at: Point };

// frontend/src/images/tools/registry.ts  (FA registers the S tool)
export function registerTool(def: ToolDefinition): void;        // throws on a duplicate id or action
export function listTools(): ToolDefinition[];                  // by `order`
export function getTool(id: ToolId): ToolDefinition | undefined;
export function activateTool(id: ToolId, api: ToolApi): void;   // deactivate old, restore type (FC-R13), activate new
export function useToolApi(ctx: CommandContext): ToolApi;       // tools/toolApi.ts
export const lastPointer: { current: Point | null };            // tools/toolApi.ts: canvas-container px of the last pointer move (T picker anchor)
// frontend/src/images/tools/types.ts
export interface ToolPointer { image: Point; screen: Point; shift: boolean; alt: boolean; button: number }
export interface ToolApi {
  store: ImagesWorkspaceStore;
  createShape(body: Omit<BoxCreate, "class_id">): Promise<BoxWriteResult | undefined>;   // adds the active type; opens T when none
  createMeasurement(a: Point, b: Point): Promise<void>;
  openPicker(purpose: "active" | "retype"): void;
  notify(text: string, tone?: "info" | "ok" | "danger"): void;
}
export interface ToolDefinition {
  id: ToolId; order: number; action: string;                    // `action` = F keymap action id
  icon: IconName; label: string; hint: string; statusHints: string;
  cursor: "default" | "crosshair" | "grab";
  drawsShapes: boolean;                                         // true: shapes ignore the pointer, the stage routes it here
  typeFilter?: (t: ClassDef) => boolean;
  available?: (s: ImagesWorkspaceState) => string | null;       // null = usable, else the reason (tooltip)
  onDown?(p: ToolPointer, api: ToolApi): void; onMove?(p: ToolPointer, api: ToolApi): void; onUp?(p: ToolPointer, api: ToolApi): void;
  onCommit?(api: ToolApi): boolean; onCancel?(api: ToolApi): boolean; onRemoveVertex?(api: ToolApi): boolean;
  renderDraft?(draft: Draft, ctx: DraftContext): ReactNode;    // DraftContext = { scale; colour; camera: CameraScale | null; image: Size }
  onActivate?(api: ToolApi): void; onDeactivate?(api: ToolApi): void;          // FA's S warms SAM here
}

// frontend/src/images/workspace/keymap.ts  (FA adds rows for 1–9, D, A, X, Shift+A/X, Tab, Shift+Tab, S, [, ])
export type KeyWhen = "always" | "idle" | "drawing" | "selection";
export interface ImagesKeyRow { keys: string[]; when: KeyWhen; action: string; help: string }
export function row(action: string, when: KeyWhen): ImagesKeyRow;        // keys + help from F's ui/keymap entry
export const IMAGES_KEY_ROWS: ImagesKeyRow[];                             // FC's and FW's rows
export function registerKeyRows(rows: ImagesKeyRow[]): void;              // FA: registerKeyRows([row("accept", "idle"), …]); throws on a collision
export const FA_ACTIONS: readonly string[];                               // the eleven actions FA binds (incl. severity 1–9, FA's R-FA3)
export function whenOverlaps(a: KeyWhen, b: KeyWhen): boolean;
export function findRowCollisions(rows: readonly ImagesKeyRow[]): Array<{ chord: string; a: ImagesKeyRow; b: ImagesKeyRow }>;
export function whenMatches(when: KeyWhen, s: Pick<ImagesWorkspaceState, "draft" | "selectedIds" | "selectedMeasurementId">): boolean;
export type KeyHandler = (chord: string) => boolean | void;               // false = not mine, try the next layer
export type KeyHandlers = Partial<Record<string, KeyHandler>>;            // keyed by action id
export function useImagesKeymap(layers: readonly KeyHandlers[], opts?: { enabled?: boolean; rows?: readonly ImagesKeyRow[] }): void;
export function useHeldKeys(): void;                                      // Space / Shift / Alt → store
export function statusHintsFor(tool: ToolId): string;                     // FW's status bar, left side

// frontend/src/images/canvas/useCanvasKeys.ts  (FW passes it as the first layer)
export function useCanvasKeyHandlers(ctx: CommandContext): KeyHandlers;

// frontend/src/images/tools/measure.ts  (FW's MeasuredSize, the L tool)
export interface CameraScale { gsdMm: number; distanceM: number; sigmaM: number; source: string }
export function scaleFromCamera(camera: ImageDetail["camera"] | null | undefined): CameraScale | null;
export function polygonArea(points: readonly Point[]): number;
export function convexHull(points: readonly Point[]): Point[];
export function feret(points: readonly Point[]): { max: number; min: number };
export function sigmaLengthMm(lengthMm: number, s: CameraScale): number;  // L·σD/D + √2·gsd
export function sigmaAreaM2(areaM2: number, s: CameraScale): number;      // 2A·σD/D
export function formatLength(mm: number): string;
export function formatArea(m2: number): string;
export function lengthLabel(px: number, s: CameraScale | null): string;   // "412 mm ± 6 mm" | "231 px"
export interface MeasuredValue { label: string; value: string; sigma: string | null }
export interface MeasuredTiles { primary: MeasuredValue | null; secondary: MeasuredValue[]; basis: string | null; pointMarker: boolean }
export function measureShape(shape: Pick<Box, "shape" | "w" | "h" | "points">, s: CameraScale | null): MeasuredTiles;

// frontend/src/images/tools/ToolPalette.tsx, ZoomCluster.tsx  (FW places them as ImageCanvas children)
export function ToolPalette(props: { ctx: CommandContext; className?: string; children?: ReactNode }): JSX.Element; // children: FA's AI button
export function ZoomCluster(props: { className?: string }): JSX.Element;   // − % + Fit, Keep zoom menu

**Interfaces consumed:**
- **I-C0 (on `main` before the cut; names from its plan):** regenerated `@contract/client` with
  `Box.{shape: BoxShape, points, assist, area_px, updated_at}`, `BoxWriteResult` (= `Box` +
  `repaired` + `finding_id`, answered by `createBox` 201 and `updateBox` 200),
  `BoxCreate.{class_id, shape?, x?, y?, w?, h?, angle?, points?, assist?}`, `BoxUpdate.points?`,
  `BoxReviewResult.{updated, finding_ids_created, finding_ids_deleted}`, `ImageDetail` (`Image` +
  `camera: ImageCamera` + `footprint` + `footprint_kind`) answered by `getImage`, `ImageCamera.{gsd_mm,
  distance_m, distance_sigma_m, distance_source: manual|lrf|rel_alt|none, …}`,
  `ImageMeasurement{id, image_id, x1, y1, x2, y2, label: string, created_at, length_px, length_mm | null,
  sigma_mm | null}`, `ImageMeasurementCreate{x1, y1, x2, y2, label?}`, `ImageMeasurementList{items}`,
  the paths `…/images/{imageId}/measurements` (GET, POST) and
  `…/image-measurements/{imageMeasurementId}` (DELETE). Task 1 Step 1 confirms them on `main`.
- **I-BA (runtime only, not needed to merge):** the shape semantics FC relies on — R-BA4 (lengths
  in px; the canvas computes mm from `ImageDetail.camera`, so it ignores `length_mm`),
  R-BA5 (`shape` for rectangles follows the angle), R-BA6 (a polygon is patched with `points` only;
  a point with `x, y` only), `422 point_needs_defect_type`, `422 empty_polygon`,
  `409 finding_would_be_deleted` (F), `409 finding_has_content` (unreview).
- **I-BK (runtime only):** `ImageDetail.camera` values; before BK merges they are Prism examples
  or `null`, and FC shows px.
- **Foundation (on `main`):** `ui/keymap.ts` (`keysFor`, `useToolShortcuts`, `isTypingTarget`,
  `chordOf`, `normaliseChord`, `WORKSPACE_KEYS.images` — already lists B R P S M L D G Shift+H N C
  arrows 0 Ctrl+1 Ctrl+D [ ] Ctrl+[ Ctrl+] Shift+M), `ui/` primitives (`FloatingToolbar`,
  `ToolButton`, `ToolSeparator`, `GlassPanel`, `Popover`, `ComboboxList`, `TypeChip`, `Dialog`,
  `Button`, `Menu`/`MenuButton`, `Kbd`, `toast`, `dur`, `easing`, `isReducedMotion`), `api/boxes.ts`
  (`fetchBoxes`, `deleteBox`), `api/findings.ts` (`fetchFinding`, `FindingDetail`), `api/errors.ts`
  (`ApiFailure`, `codeOf`, `messageOf`, `unwrap`), `api/client.tsx` (`useApi`, `ApiContext`),
  `app/diagnostics.ts` (`pushLog`), `store/changes.ts` (`boxesRevision`), `test/fixtures.ts`
  (`fakeClient`, `personBox`, `proposalBox`, `exampleClasses`, `exampleImage`, `PROJECT_ID`,
  `IMAGE_ID`, `CLASS_ID`, `errorBody`, `FakeRoute`).

**Gate (Task 11, from `E:\Dev\Yolo\app\.claude\worktrees\i-fc`):**

```
pnpm -C contract check

## Global Constraints

- **Worktree.** Build only in `E:\Dev\Yolo\app\.claude\worktrees\i-fc`. The worktree has no venv
  and may have no `node_modules`: run `pnpm -C frontend install --frozen-lockfile` once if
  `frontend\node_modules` is missing. Do not use `scripts/start-task.ps1` / `finish-task.ps1`.
- **Contract.** FC never edits `contract/`. Types come from `@contract/client`
  (`components["schemas"][…]`). A name that differs from this plan is corrected from the merged
  contract (Task 1), never by editing the contract.
- **World coordinates** are stored-image pixels (the space of `image.width/height`), as today.
  Every point sent to the API is in that space, rounded to 0.1 px.
- **Design system.** Only `frontend/src/ui/` primitives and DESIGN.md tokens; no raw palette,
  arbitrary colours, `backdrop-*`, `duration-<n>`, arbitrary radii/fonts/shadows, or
  `motion-reduce:` (use `reduce-motion:`). Konva colours come from data (`ClassDef.colour`) or from
  `canvasColour(token, alpha)` (Task 7), which reads the RGB-triplet tokens (`--ok` is the teal,
  `--accent` the violet). `node scripts/check-tokens.mjs` stays green.
- **Motion (§12, A6).** F's tokens only (`dur.fast` 120, `dur.base` 180, `dur.slow` 260; `easing`).
  Nothing on an interaction path longer than 400 ms. No loop on the canvas; no marching ants; the
  selection glow is static. Under reduced motion (`isReducedMotion()`), view tweens are instant
  and the image fade uses `dur.fast` (the one duration reduced motion keeps).
- **Keys (§13, I-D9).** Digits are severity (FA binds them, its R-FA3), letters choose tools, T opens the type picker, X
  rejects (FA). Keys never fire in text fields (`isTypingTarget`); a focused primitive keeps its own
  keys. Every chord FC binds is an entry of F's `keysFor("images")`.
- **Values copied from the spec:** polygon streaming every **4 screen px**; LOD Douglas–Peucker at
  **0.75 screen px** per zoom bucket (powers of **√2**); listening resumes **120 ms** after the last
  wheel/drag; preview `max_side=`**2048**; full swap once `scale > 2048 / long side` or **400 ms**
  after navigation; LRU **1 full + 4 previews**; labels only on selected/hovered shapes or shapes
  **≥ 48 screen px** tall; rotate snap **15°** with Shift; measure constrain **0/45/90°**; nudge
  **1 px** (Shift **10 px**); rotate **1°**; polygon ≤ **2,000** vertices; threshold step **0.05**;
  σ_L = L·σ_D/D + √2·gsd; σ_A ≈ 2A·σ_D/D.
- **Localstorage** (last type per tool per project) is wrapped in try/catch and optional.
- **Commits.** Stage by path, never `git add -A`. End every message with
  `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Focused tests per task** (`pnpm -C frontend exec vitest run <files>`); the full gate once, in
  Task 11.

## Rulings (the spec left these open; decided here)

- **FC-R1 Where the editor modules go.** `geometry.ts` and `history.ts` are `git mv`ed into
  `images/canvas/` and the old paths become one-line re-export shims, so the interim Editor keeps
  compiling unchanged and FW deletes `editor/` wholesale. `editor/commands.ts`, `BoxLayer.tsx`,
  `EditorCanvas.tsx`, `useKonvaImage.ts` and `store/editor.ts` are **not** edited: they are bound to
  `store/editor.ts`, which the interim screen still uses. Their logic is ported (with the tests'
  intent) into the new files: `commands.ts` → `images/canvas/commands.ts`, `BoxLayer.tsx`'s
  centre-pivoted Rect + Transformer + 15° snaps → `ShapeLayer.tsx`/`InteractionLayer.tsx`,
  `EditorCanvas.tsx`'s window-level middle-button pan and wheel zoom → `ImageCanvas.tsx` (the pan
  block is copied verbatim, including its comments), `useKonvaImage.ts` → `useTwoLevelImage.ts`.
- **FC-R2 Commands are generalised, not renamed-only.** `cmdCreateBox` → `cmdCreateShape` (any
  `shape`); `cmdUpdateRect` → `cmdUpdateShape` (rect, polygon points, or point position);
  `cmdSetClass` → `cmdSetType` (many ids, `confirm_finding_delete` flow); `cmdReview` keeps its name
  and returns the `BoxReviewResult` so FA can open `finding_ids_created[0]`; `cmdDelete` →
  `cmdDeleteShapes` (many ids). `cmdToggleEmpty` is **not** ported (N is FW's, spec §13).
- **FC-R3 Undo across a finding.** Undo of a create deletes the box (F deletes its finding). Undo of
  a delete re-creates the shape (a **new** finding for a defect type; the old finding's note, photos
  and comments are gone, which the delete confirmation says). Undo of a retype re-types with
  `confirm_finding_delete=true` (the person consented to the original change). Undo of an accept
  that answers `409 finding_has_content` shows spec §16's message "This finding has a note or
  photos; delete it from the inspector." and leaves the history entry in place.
- **FC-R4 Delete confirmation.** `Del` on a selection that includes shapes with a known finding
  link (`findingOf`, FC-R16) reads each finding (`fetchFinding`); if any has content (`note.trim() !== ""`,
  `attachment_count > 0` or `comment_count > 0`) a `Dialog` confirms: "Delete N shapes? F-0217 has
  a note, photos or comments. Deleting the shape deletes its finding; undo brings the shape back
  but not the finding's note, photos or comments." A proposal (non-person provenance) is rejected
  rather than deleted, as today.
- **FC-R5 Retype confirmation.** `cmdSetType` answers `"needs-confirm"` on
  `409 finding_would_be_deleted`; `actions.retypeSelection` then opens a `Dialog`: "Change to an
  object type? The finding F-… is deleted with its note, photos and comments." Confirm retries the
  ids with `confirm_finding_delete=true`.
- **FC-R6 Point markers.** The M tool offers defect types only (`typeFilter: kind === "defect"`).
  With no defect type active, a click opens the T picker (purpose `active`) at the cursor and
  toasts "Point markers need a defect type. Pick one." The server's `422 point_needs_defect_type`
  is shown as the error text if it happens anyway.
- **FC-R7 Multi-selection.** Shift-click toggles a shape in `selectedIds`. Delete, Ctrl+D and T act
  on every selected shape. The Transformer, vertex handles, drag-move, nudge and rotate act only
  when exactly one shape is selected (a drag on an unselected shape selects it alone first). No
  marquee.
- **FC-R8 Layer 3 is a slot.** FC owns the Konva `Layer` named `suggestions` (so the listening and
  visibility rules live in one place) and renders `props.suggestions` inside it. FA's suggestion
  layer supplies the nodes, reusing `ShapeNode` with `variant="suggestion"` (static teal dash). With
  no slot content, unreviewed shapes are not drawn. Rejected shapes are never drawn.
- **FC-R9 Image change.** The new image fades in over `dur.fast` (120 ms) on the canvas background,
  instead of a crossfade from the old frame: a crossfade would draw the outgoing frame in the new
  image's world coordinates (different sizes) and hold a second full bitmap past the LRU budget.
  The view refits unless **Keep zoom** (the zoom cluster's menu) is on, in which case scale and
  centre are kept when both images have the same size, and refit otherwise.
- **FC-R10 No new or changed `ui/` primitive.** `ToolButton`'s tooltip shows the name and the key
  ("Measure length · L"); the spec's tooltip sub-line ("mm from GSD") is carried in
  `ToolDefinition.hint` (for the ? sheet and a later tooltip), the status bar shows
  `statusHints`, and the tooltip keeps F's one-line form. If the
  controller later adds an optional `hint` prop to `ToolButton`, the palette passes `def.hint`
  (one line in `ToolPalette.tsx`). Icons reuse F's gallery mapping: select `fit`, pan `map`, box
  `label`, rbox `refresh`, polygon `drawing`, point `pin`, length `measure`, delete `trash`.
- **FC-R11 Keymap mechanics.** The `when` of a row is checked at key time against the store
  (`whenMatches`), not at bind time. `useImagesKeymap` binds **one** `useToolShortcuts` entry per
  chord (with F's action for that chord, so F's global/review guard accepts it) and dispatches to
  the first handler layer whose handler for that action does not return `false`. Space, Shift and
  Alt "held" state comes from `useHeldKeys` (a layout-effect keydown/keyup listener, the
  `useEditorHotkeys` pattern — `vault/decisions` CI flake note), not from `useToolShortcuts`.
  `useToolShortcuts` ignores key repeat, so holding Alt+→ nudges once per press (accepted).
- **FC-R12 Seeing `ImageCanvas` without the route.** A dev-only page `frontend/canvas-lab.html`
  (like `gallery.html`, not part of `vite build`, which builds `index.html` only) mounts
  `images/canvas/lab/CanvasLab.tsx` inside an `ApiContext.Provider` whose client is the in-memory
  lab API (`lab/labApi.ts`, built on `fakeClient`). It draws a generated 4000×3000 test frame
  (an `OffscreenCanvas`/`<canvas>` → blob URL), seeds shapes, and takes `?shapes=500` (300 polygons
  of 40 vertices + 200 boxes, the §17 flow-6 load) so I-E's perf harness can reuse it. Open it at
  `http://127.0.0.1:1420/canvas-lab.html` under `pnpm -C frontend dev`. It is not a gallery section
  because the gallery is F's primitives page and has no API context.
- **FC-R13 Active type.** The active type is per tool: `setTool` restores the last type used with
  that tool in this project (`typeMemory`, `localStorage` key `kestrel.images.lastType.<projectId>`,
  a JSON `{toolId: typeId}`), falling back to the current type if the tool's `typeFilter` accepts
  it, else the first type the filter accepts, else `null`.
- **FC-R14 Length measurement.** The L tool posts `{x1, y1, x2, y2}` in image px; the canvas shows
  the live label with `lengthLabel(px, scaleFromCamera(image.camera))` next to the line, and saved
  measurements on layer 2 with the same label. `MeasuredSize` (the inspector tiles) is FW's and
  calls `measureShape`. Selecting a measurement is a click on its line in the select tool; `Del`
  deletes it (undo re-creates it).
- **FC-R16 Finding links live in the store.** `Box` carries no finding id (C0 ruling 1), so the
  store keeps `findingOf: Record<boxId, findingId>`: `cmdCreateShape` records `finding_id` from its
  `BoxWriteResult` (and re-records it on redo / undo-of-delete), FA calls `linkFindings` after an
  accept (`finding_ids_created`), and FW calls it with the image's `GET /findings?image_id=` list.
  It resets per image. A shape without a known link is deleted without a confirmation (the server
  still deletes its finding, A3); once FW links the list, every defect shape is covered.
- **FC-R15 Save failure and retry.** `tracked` records `failure = {message, retry}` where `retry`
  re-runs the same API call and, on success, applies the same store update; FW renders "▲ Save
  failed · Retry" from `saveState(s) === "failed"` and `s.failure.retry`.

## Deviations from the spec (code on `main` differs)

- **D1** `editor/useKonvaImage.ts` is not "extended": the interim Editor still imports it, so the
  two-level loader is a new hook (`useTwoLevelImage`) and FW deletes the old one.
- **D2** Spec §13 says I's keys are "registered into F's keymap". F already registered them: on
  `main`, `WORKSPACE_KEYS.images` in `ui/keymap.ts` lists every I tool key, and F's
  `keymap.test.tsx` already checks them against the global and review keys. So FC's
  `images/workspace/keymap.ts` **annotates** F's entries (adds `when`, derives keys and help from
  F's table by action id) instead of adding entries to F's file.
- **D3** The spec's tool tooltip sub-line is not rendered in batch 2 (FC-R10).
- **D4** Image change fades the new frame in rather than crossfading (FC-R9).
- **D5** `ClassDef` has no `archived` flag on `main`; the T picker offers every type in
  `ProjectOut.classes`, which F derives from the project's active types.
- **D6** F's `nudge` entry has only `Alt+Arrow`; spec §13's "with Shift: 10 px" needs
  `Alt+Shift+Arrow`, which `chordOf` produces for Shift+Alt+→ and no entry owned. FC adds the four
  chords to F's `nudge` entry (the one shared-file touch in `ui/keymap.ts`).

## Review Focus

1. **A key pressed the instant a new image appears** must act on that image, not be dropped or
   applied to the previous one: handlers read `useImagesWorkspace.getState()` at key time, and
   `useImagesKeymap` stays enabled while loading. Test: Task 8
   `acts on the image loaded in the same tick`.
2. **Rotated box drawn right-to-left or bottom-to-top** (edge B before A on screen, width on the
   "negative" side) must give the same rectangle as the mirror stroke, with `angle` in [0, 180) and
   `h > 0`. Test: Task 6 `the reversed stroke gives the same rectangle`.
3. **A polygon closed by clicking the first vertex after a streamed drag** must not add a
   duplicate closing vertex and must not commit with < 3 distinct vertices. Test: Task 6
   `closing on the first vertex adds no vertex` and `refuses to close with two distinct vertices`.
4. **Zoomed-in far, then navigate:** the full bitmap of the old image is evicted and `close()`d
   only when it is no longer drawn; a slow full decode for the old image that lands after
   navigation is neither shown nor leaks. Test: Task 5 `never closes a pinned bitmap` and
   `a late full decode for a previous image is cached, not shown`.
5. **Undo after an accept whose finding the person already annotated** answers `409
   finding_has_content`; the canvas must show the spec message, keep the shape accepted, and keep
   the history entry so the person is not left with a lost redo. Test: Task 3
   `undo of an accept refused with finding_has_content keeps the entry`.

## Test hooks for I-E

I-E's `frontend/e2e/images/ui.ts` (plan `2026-09-27-images-e.md`, row I-FC) guessed these hooks on
components FC owns. FC emits them with E's exact names:

| Hook | Where (task) | Value |
|---|---|---|
| `data-testid="image-canvas"` | `ImageCanvas` host `div` (T10) | the Konva stage's container is inside it |
| `data-image` | same | `"{width}x{height}"` of the loaded image, `""` before one loads |
| `data-view-scale`, `data-view-x`, `data-view-y` | same | `view.scale.toFixed(4)`, `x/y.toFixed(1)` (as `editor-canvas` on `main`) |
| `data-tool` | same | the active `ToolId`: `select`, `pan`, `box`, `rbox`, `polygon`, `point`, `length` (FA: `smart`) |
| `data-shape-count` | same | shapes on the image that are not rejected (accepted + pending suggestions) |
| `data-interacting` | same (extra) | `"true"` while pan/zoom holds the layers deaf |
| Konva layer names `image`, `annotations`, `suggestions`, `interaction` | `LAYER_NAMES` (T7), set on each `<Layer name>` (T7, T10) | E's `ANNOTATION_LAYER`/`SUGGESTION_LAYER` constants match |
| type picker dialog "Choose a type" | `TypePicker` `Popover` (role `dialog`) label (T9) | same name for both purposes (active type and retype) |
| tool keys V H B R P M L, Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y | `IMAGES_KEY_ROWS` + `useCanvasKeyHandlers` (T8) | as E expects |
| palette | `data-testid="tool-palette"` on the palette wrapper; `role="toolbar"` named "Image tools"; buttons named by tool label ("Select", "Pan", "Box", "Rotated box", "Polygon", "Point marker", "Measure length", "Delete", "Hide annotations"/"Show annotations", "Hide AI suggestions"/"Show AI suggestions"); active-type button "Active type: {name}. Change (T)" (T9) | extra names E may use |
| zoom cluster | `role="toolbar"` named "Zoom"; buttons "Zoom out", "Zoom in", "Fit", "Zoom options" (T9) | extra |
| delete / retype dialogs | `Dialog` titles "Delete this shape?" / "Delete N shapes?" and "Change to an object type?" (T9) | extra |

Not FC's: `data-point-count` (FB's capture map), the info chip, status bar, save state, inspector,
measure readout (FW), the hint bar and model menu (FA). No E name conflicts with FC's design.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing geometry tests**

Append to `frontend/src/images/canvas/geometry.test.ts` (keep the existing imports; add the new
names to the import list from `./geometry`: `cornersOf`, `distance`, `snapDirection`,
`rboxFromThreePoints`, `envelopeOf`, `fromPoints`, `toPoints`, `flatten`, `nearestEdge`,
`translatePoints`, `clampPoint`, and `describe, expect, it` from vitest if not already there):

```ts
describe("three-point rotated box (I-D8)", () => {
  it("builds an axis-aligned box from an edge and a width point", () => {
    expect(rboxFromThreePoints({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 100, y: 0 }, { x: 50, y: 40 })).toEqual({
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      w: 100,
      h: 40,
      angle: 0,
    });
  });

  it("puts the width on the side of the third point", () => {
    const r = rboxFromThreePoints({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 100, y: 0 }, { x: 50, y: -40 })!;
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ x: 0, y: -40, w: 100, h: 40, angle: 0 });
  });

  it("keeps the drawn edge as a real edge at 90 degrees", () => {
    const r = rboxFromThreePoints({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: 100 }, { x: -30, y: 50 })!;
    expect(r.angle).toBeCloseTo(90);
    expect(r.w).toBeCloseTo(100);
    expect(r.h).toBeCloseTo(30);
    const corners = cornersOf(r);
    expect(corners.some((c) => Math.abs(c.x) < 1e-9 && Math.abs(c.y) < 1e-9)).toBe(true);
    expect(corners.some((c) => Math.abs(c.x) < 1e-9 && Math.abs(c.y - 100) < 1e-9)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("the reversed stroke gives the same rectangle", () => {
    const a = { x: 10, y: 20 };
    const b = { x: 110, y: 78 };
    const c = { x: 80, y: 120 };
    const forward = rboxFromThreePoints(a, b, c)!;
    const reversed = rboxFromThreePoints(b, a, c)!;
    expect(reversed.angle).toBeCloseTo(forward.angle);
    expect(reversed.angle).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(reversed.angle).toBeLessThan(180);
    for (const k of ["x", "y", "w", "h"] as const) expect(reversed[k]).toBeCloseTo(forward[k]);
    expect(forward.h).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("refuses a degenerate edge or width", () => {
    expect(rboxFromThreePoints({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 1, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: 30 })).toBeNull();
    expect(rboxFromThreePoints({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 100, y: 0 }, { x: 50, y: 1 })).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("snaps", () => {
  it("snaps a direction to the step and keeps the length", () => {
    const b = snapDirection({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 100, y: 12 }, 15);
    expect(b.y).toBeCloseTo(0);
    expect(distance({ x: 0, y: 0 }, b)).toBeCloseTo(Math.hypot(100, 12));
    const diag = snapDirection({ x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 50, y: 47 }, 45);
    expect(diag.x).toBeCloseTo(diag.y);
  });
});

describe("polygon helpers", () => {
  const square = [
    { x: 10, y: 10 },
    { x: 50, y: 10 },
    { x: 50, y: 30 },
    { x: 10, y: 30 },
  ];

  it("round-trips API points and flattens for Konva", () => {
    expect(toPoints([[1, 2], [3, 4]])).toEqual([{ x: 1, y: 2 }, { x: 3, y: 4 }]);
    expect(fromPoints([{ x: 1.04, y: 2.06 }])).toEqual([[1, 2.1]]);
    expect(flatten(square)).toEqual([10, 10, 50, 10, 50, 30, 10, 30]);
  });

  it("gives the envelope", () => {
    expect(envelopeOf(square)).toEqual({ x: 10, y: 10, w: 40, h: 20 });
  });

  it("finds the nearest edge and the foot point", () => {
    const hit = nearestEdge(square, { x: 30, y: 12 })!;
    expect(hit.index).toBe(0);
    expect(hit.distance).toBeCloseTo(2);
    expect(hit.at).toEqual({ x: 30, y: 10 });
    const closing = nearestEdge(square, { x: 8, y: 20 })!;
    expect(closing.index).toBe(3); // the edge from the last vertex back to the first
  });

  it("translates and clamps", () => {
    expect(translatePoints(square, 5, -5)[0]).toEqual({ x: 15, y: 5 });
    expect(clampPoint({ x: -3, y: 120 }, { width: 100, height: 100 })).toEqual({ x: 0, y: 100 });
  });
});
```

Append to `frontend/src/images/canvas/geometry.ts`:

```ts
export function distance(a: Point, b: Point): number {
  return Math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.y - a.y);
}

export function snapAngle(deg: number, step: number): number {
  return Math.round(deg / step) * step;
}

/** `b` moved so the direction a→b is a multiple of `step` degrees; the length is kept. */
export function snapDirection(a: Point, b: Point, step: number): Point {
  const len = distance(a, b);
  if (len === 0) return b;
  const deg = snapAngle((Math.atan2(b.y - a.y, b.x - a.x) * 180) / Math.PI, step);
  const rad = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;
  return { x: a.x + len * Math.cos(rad), y: a.y + len * Math.sin(rad) };
}

/**
 * The rotated box of the three-point gesture (spec I-D8): a→b is one edge (its direction is the
 * angle, its length `w`), and the signed distance of `c` from that edge's line is the width `h`,
 * on c's side. `null` when either side is shorter than MIN_BOX_SIDE. The result is an
 * OrientedRect (x, y, w, h of the unrotated box, angle in [0, 180)).
 */
export function rboxFromThreePoints(a: Point, b: Point, c: Point): OrientedRect | null {
  const dx = b.x - a.x;
  const dy = b.y - a.y;
  const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
  if (len < MIN_BOX_SIDE) return null;
  const nx = -dy / len;
  const ny = dx / len;
  const d = (c.x - a.x) * nx + (c.y - a.y) * ny;
  if (Math.abs(d) < MIN_BOX_SIDE) return null;
  const cx = (a.x + b.x) / 2 + (nx * d) / 2;
  const cy = (a.y + b.y) / 2 + (ny * d) / 2;
  const h = Math.abs(d);
  return { x: cx - len / 2, y: cy - h / 2, w: len, h, angle: normaliseAngle((Math.atan2(dy, dx) * 180) / Math.PI) };
}

export function envelopeOf(points: readonly Point[]): Rect {
  const xs = points.map((p) => p.x);
  const ys = points.map((p) => p.y);
  const x = Math.min(...xs);
  const y = Math.min(...ys);
  return { x, y, w: Math.max(...xs) - x, h: Math.max(...ys) - y };
}

/** API polygon points (`[[x, y], …]`) to Points. */
export function toPoints(raw: readonly (readonly number[])[]): Point[] {
  return raw.map(([x, y]) => ({ x, y }));
}

/** Points to API polygon points, rounded (0.1 px by default, the server's precision). */
export function fromPoints(points: readonly Point[], decimals = 1): number[][] {
  const f = 10 ** decimals;
  return points.map((p) => [Math.round(p.x * f) / f, Math.round(p.y * f) / f]);
}

/** Konva's flat `[x0, y0, x1, y1, …]`. */
export function flatten(points: readonly Point[]): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (const p of points) out.push(p.x, p.y);
  return out;
}

export function distanceToSegment(p: Point, a: Point, b: Point): { distance: number; at: Point } {
  const dx = b.x - a.x;
  const dy = b.y - a.y;
  const len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
  const t = len2 === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((p.x - a.x) * dx + (p.y - a.y) * dy) / len2));
  const at = { x: a.x + t * dx, y: a.y + t * dy };
  return { distance: distance(p, at), at };
}

/** The closed ring's edge nearest to `p`; edge `i` runs from points[i] to points[(i + 1) % n]. */
export function nearestEdge(
  points: readonly Point[],
  p: Point,
): { index: number; distance: number; at: Point } | null {
  if (points.length < 2) return null;
  let best: { index: number; distance: number; at: Point } | null = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
    const hit = distanceToSegment(p, points[i], points[(i + 1) % points.length]);
    if (!best || hit.distance < best.distance) best = { index: i, ...hit };
  }
  return best;
}

export function translatePoints(points: readonly Point[], dx: number, dy: number): Point[] {
  return points.map((p) => ({ x: p.x + dx, y: p.y + dy }));
}

export function clampPoint(p: Point, image: Size): Point {
  return { x: Math.min(Math.max(p.x, 0), image.width), y: Math.min(Math.max(p.y, 0), image.height) };
}
```

import type { Box } from "@contract/client";
import type { BoxWriteResult, ImageDetail, ImageMeasurement } from "@/api/shapes";
import { exampleImage, IMAGE_ID, personBox } from "@/test/fixtures";

/** A Box with every shape field set; `partial` wins. */
export function makeShape(partial: Partial<Box> = {}): Box {
  const base: Box = {
    ...personBox,
    shape: "box",
    points: null,
    assist: null,
    area_px: personBox.w * personBox.h,
    updated_at: personBox.created_at,
  };
  return { ...base, ...partial };
}

/** What create and update answer (C0 ruling 1): the Box plus `repaired` and `finding_id`. */
export function makeWritten(partial: Partial<BoxWriteResult> = {}): BoxWriteResult {
  return { ...makeShape(partial), repaired: false, finding_id: null, ...partial };
}

/** An ImageDetail at 4000 × 3000 with a nadir camera at 40 m and GSD 2 mm/px. */
export function makeDetail(partial: Partial<ImageDetail> = {}): ImageDetail {
  const base = {
    ...exampleImage,
    width: 4000,
    height: 3000,
    camera: {
      rel_alt: 40,
      gimbal_pitch: -90,
      gimbal_yaw: 0,
      focal_mm: 12.29,
      focal_px: null,
      sensor_w_mm: 17.3,
      lrf_distance_m: null,
      subject_distance_m: null,
      distance_m: 40,
      distance_sigma_m: 1,
      distance_source: "rel_alt",
      gsd_mm: 2,
      camera_model: "M3E",
    },
    footprint: null,
    footprint_kind: "none",
  };
  return { ...base, ...partial } as ImageDetail;
}

export function makeMeasurement(partial: Partial<ImageMeasurement> = {}): ImageMeasurement {
  const base = {
    id: "me000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001",
    image_id: IMAGE_ID,
    x1: 100,
    y1: 100,
    x2: 400,
    y2: 500,
    label: "",
    length_px: 500,
    length_mm: null,
    sigma_mm: null,
    created_at: "2026-09-27T10:00:00Z",
  };
  return { ...base, ...partial } as ImageMeasurement;
}

describe("waitForIdle", () => {

    st().beginRequest();
    let done = false;

    await Promise.resolve();

  });

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the tween**

Create `frontend/src/images/canvas/viewTween.ts`:

```ts
import type { ViewTransform } from "./geometry";
import { cubicBezier, easing } from "@/ui/motion";

const ease = cubicBezier(...easing.out);
/** rAF in the app; a 16 ms timer where there is none (jsdom), so fake timers can drive it. */
const frame = (cb: () => void): (() => void) => {
  if (typeof requestAnimationFrame === "function" && !import.meta.env?.VITEST) {
    const id = requestAnimationFrame(cb);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(id);
  }
  const id = setTimeout(cb, 16);
  return () => clearTimeout(id);
};

/**
 * Tweens the view from `from` to `to` over `ms` with --ease-out, calling `apply` each frame and
 * once more with `to` exactly. Returns a cancel function; a cancelled tween stops where it is.
 */
export function animateView(
  from: ViewTransform,
  to: ViewTransform,
  ms: number,
  apply: (v: ViewTransform) => void,
): () => void {
  const start = Date.now();
  let cancel: () => void = () => undefined;
  let stopped = false;
  const step = () => {
    if (stopped) return;
    const t = Math.min(1, (Date.now() - start) / ms);
    const k = ease(t);
    if (t >= 1) {
      apply(to);
      return;
    }
    // Interpolate the scale geometrically so zooming feels even at every level.
    const scale = from.scale * (to.scale / from.scale) ** k;
    apply({ scale, x: from.x + (to.x - from.x) * k, y: from.y + (to.y - from.y) * k });
    cancel = frame(step);
  };
  cancel = frame(step);
  return () => {
    stopped = true;
    cancel();
  };
}
```

```ts
import { create, type StoreApi, type UseBoundStore } from "zustand";
import type { Box, ClassDef, ReviewState } from "@contract/client";
import type { FindingDetail } from "@/api/findings";
import type { ImageDetail, ImageMeasurement } from "@/api/shapes";
import {
  clampScale,
  fitView,
  oneToOneView,
  toImage,
  zoomAround,
  ZOOM_STEP,
  type Point,
  type Rect,
  type Size,
  type ViewTransform,
} from "@/images/canvas/geometry";
import { History } from "@/images/canvas/history";
import { animateView } from "@/images/canvas/viewTween";
import { dur, isReducedMotion } from "@/ui/motion";

export type ToolId = "select" | "pan" | "box" | "rbox" | "polygon" | "point" | "length" | (string & {});

export type Draft =
  | { kind: "rect"; anchor: Point; start: Point; rect: Rect | null }
  | { kind: "rbox"; stage: "edge" | "width"; a: Point; b: Point; c: Point | null }
  | { kind: "polygon"; points: Point[]; cursor: Point | null; pressed: boolean; lastScreen: Point | null }
  | { kind: "length"; a: Point; b: Point | null }
  | { kind: "custom"; tool: string; data: unknown };

export type ConfirmState =
  | { kind: "delete"; ids: string[]; findings: FindingDetail[] }
  | { kind: "retype"; ids: string[]; typeId: string }
  | null;

export const INITIAL_VIEW: ViewTransform = { scale: 1, x: 0, y: 0 };
export const THRESHOLD_STEP = 0.05;

export interface ImagesWorkspaceState {
  projectId: string | null;
  imageId: string | null;
  image: ImageDetail | null;
  types: ClassDef[];
  boxes: Record<string, Box>;
  order: string[];
  measurements: Record<string, ImageMeasurement>;
  selectedIds: string[];
  selectedMeasurementId: string | null;
  hoveredId: string | null;
  focusedSuggestionId: string | null;
  tool: ToolId;
  activeTypeId: string | null;
  draft: Draft | null;
  view: ViewTransform;
  viewport: Size;
  fitted: boolean;
  keepZoom: boolean;
  interacting: boolean;
  spaceHeld: boolean;
  shiftHeld: boolean;
  altHeld: boolean;
  showAnnotations: boolean;
  showSuggestions: boolean;
  threshold: number;
  history: History;
  pending: number;
  failure: { message: string; retry: (() => void) | null } | null;
  picker: { at: Point; purpose: "retype" | "active" } | null;
  confirm: ConfirmState;
  /** Box id → finding id; `Box` carries no link (C0 ruling 1, FC-R16). */
  findingOf: Record<string, string>;

  setProject: (projectId: string) => void;
  loadImage: (image: ImageDetail, boxes: Box[], measurements: ImageMeasurement[]) => void;
  setImage: (image: ImageDetail) => void;
  setTypes: (types: readonly ClassDef[]) => void;
  setBoxes: (boxes: Box[]) => void;
  upsertBox: (box: Box) => void;
  removeBox: (id: string) => void;
  patchStates: (ids: string[], state: ReviewState) => void;
  upsertMeasurement: (m: ImageMeasurement) => void;
  removeMeasurement: (id: string) => void;
  select: (ids: string[], mode?: "replace" | "toggle") => void;
  selectMeasurement: (id: string | null) => void;
  hover: (id: string | null) => void;
  focusSuggestion: (id: string | null) => void;
  setTool: (tool: ToolId) => void;
  setActiveType: (id: string | null) => void;
  setDraft: (draft: Draft | null) => void;
  setViewport: (size: Size) => void;
  setView: (view: ViewTransform) => void;
  fit: () => void;
  oneToOne: () => void;
  zoomAt: (display: Point, factor: number) => void;
  zoomBy: (factor: number) => void;
  centreOn: (point: Point, opts?: { radiusPx?: number; animate?: boolean }) => void;
  panIntoView: (rect: Rect, opts?: { animate?: boolean }) => void;
  setKeepZoom: (on: boolean) => void;
  setInteracting: (on: boolean) => void;
  setHeld: (keys: Partial<{ space: boolean; shift: boolean; alt: boolean }>) => void;
  toggleAnnotations: () => void;
  toggleSuggestions: () => void;
  setThreshold: (value: number) => void;
  beginRequest: () => void;
  endRequest: () => void;
  fail: (message: string, retry?: () => void) => void;
  clearFailure: () => void;
  openPicker: (at: Point, purpose: "retype" | "active") => void;
  closePicker: () => void;
  setConfirm: (c: ConfirmState) => void;
  linkFindings: (links: Record<string, string>) => void;
  unlinkFinding: (boxId: string) => void;
  reset: () => void;
}

function sortedIds(boxes: Record<string, Box>): string[] {
  return Object.values(boxes)
    .sort((a, b) => a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id))
    .map((b) => b.id);
}

const keyed = <T extends { id: string }>(rows: readonly T[]): Record<string, T> =>
  Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));

/** Everything that belongs to one image; `pending` and `failure` survive navigation on purpose. */
const PER_IMAGE = {
  selectedIds: [] as string[],
  selectedMeasurementId: null,
  hoveredId: null,
  focusedSuggestionId: null,
  draft: null,
  picker: null,
  confirm: null,
  findingOf: {} as Record<string, string>,
} satisfies Partial<ImagesWorkspaceState>;

let cancelTween: (() => void) | null = null;

export const useImagesWorkspace = create<ImagesWorkspaceState>((set, get) => {
  /** Every view change goes through here so a running tween never fights a new one. */
  const moveTo = (target: ViewTransform, animate = false) => {
    cancelTween?.();
    cancelTween = null;
    if (!animate || isReducedMotion()) {
      set({ view: target });
      return;
    }
    cancelTween = animateView(get().view, target, dur.slow, (v) => set({ view: v }));
  };

  return {
    projectId: null,
    imageId: null,
    image: null,
    types: [],
    boxes: {},
    order: [],
    measurements: {},
    ...PER_IMAGE,
    tool: "select",
    activeTypeId: null,
    view: INITIAL_VIEW,
    viewport: { width: 0, height: 0 },
    fitted: false,
    keepZoom: false,
    interacting: false,
    spaceHeld: false,
    shiftHeld: false,
    altHeld: false,
    showAnnotations: true,
    showSuggestions: true,
    threshold: 0,
    history: new History(),
    pending: 0,
    failure: null,

    setProject: (projectId) => set((s) => (s.projectId === projectId ? s : { projectId })),
    loadImage: (image, boxes, measurements) =>
      set((s) => {
        const map = keyed(boxes);
        const canFit = s.viewport.width > 0 && s.viewport.height > 0;
        const keep =
          s.keepZoom && s.fitted && s.image !== null && s.image.width === image.width && s.image.height === image.height;
        cancelTween?.();
        return {
          ...PER_IMAGE,
          imageId: image.id,
          image,
          boxes: map,
          order: sortedIds(map),
          measurements: keyed(measurements),
          history: new History(),
          view: keep ? s.view : canFit ? fitView(image, s.viewport) : INITIAL_VIEW,
          fitted: keep || canFit,
        };
      }),
    // A response for an image the workspace has since left must not land here.
    setImage: (image) => set((s) => (s.imageId === image.id ? { image } : s)),
    setTypes: (types) => set({ types: [...types] }),
    setBoxes: (boxes) =>
      set((s) => {
        const map = keyed(boxes.filter((b) => b.image_id === s.imageId));
        return {
          boxes: map,
          order: sortedIds(map),
          selectedIds: s.selectedIds.filter((id) => map[id]),
          focusedSuggestionId: s.focusedSuggestionId && map[s.focusedSuggestionId] ? s.focusedSuggestionId : null,
        };
      }),
    upsertBox: (box) =>
      set((s) => {
        if (s.imageId !== null && box.image_id !== s.imageId) return s;
        const boxes = { ...s.boxes, [box.id]: box };
        return { boxes, order: s.boxes[box.id] ? s.order : sortedIds(boxes) };
      }),
    removeBox: (id) =>
      set((s) => {
        if (!s.boxes[id]) return s;
        const boxes = { ...s.boxes };
        delete boxes[id];
        return {
          boxes,
          order: s.order.filter((x) => x !== id),
          selectedIds: s.selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== id),
          hoveredId: s.hoveredId === id ? null : s.hoveredId,
          focusedSuggestionId: s.focusedSuggestionId === id ? null : s.focusedSuggestionId,
        };
      }),
    patchStates: (ids, state) =>
      set((s) => {
        const reviewedAt = state === "unreviewed" ? null : new Date().toISOString();
        const boxes = { ...s.boxes };
        for (const id of ids) if (boxes[id]) boxes[id] = { ...boxes[id], review_state: state, reviewed_at: reviewedAt };
        return { boxes };
      }),
    upsertMeasurement: (m) =>
      set((s) => (s.imageId !== null && m.image_id !== s.imageId ? s : { measurements: { ...s.measurements, [m.id]: m } })),
    removeMeasurement: (id) =>
      set((s) => {
        const measurements = { ...s.measurements };
        delete measurements[id];
        return { measurements, selectedMeasurementId: s.selectedMeasurementId === id ? null : s.selectedMeasurementId };
      }),
    select: (ids, mode = "replace") =>
      set((s) => {
        const known = ids.filter((id) => s.boxes[id]);
        let next: string[];
        if (mode === "toggle") {
          next = [...s.selectedIds];
          for (const id of known) next = next.includes(id) ? next.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...next, id];
        } else next = known;
        return { selectedIds: next, selectedMeasurementId: next.length ? null : s.selectedMeasurementId };
      }),
    selectMeasurement: (id) =>
      set((s) => (id !== null && !s.measurements[id] ? s : { selectedMeasurementId: id, selectedIds: id ? [] : s.selectedIds })),
    hover: (id) => set((s) => (s.hoveredId === id ? s : { hoveredId: id })),
    focusSuggestion: (id) => set({ focusedSuggestionId: id }),
    setTool: (tool) => set((s) => (s.tool === tool ? s : { tool, draft: null })),
    setActiveType: (id) => set({ activeTypeId: id }),
    setDraft: (draft) => set({ draft }),
    setViewport: (size) =>
      set((s) => {
        if (size.width === s.viewport.width && size.height === s.viewport.height) return s;
        if (s.image && !s.fitted && size.width > 0 && size.height > 0) {
          return { viewport: size, view: fitView(s.image, size), fitted: true };
        }
        return { viewport: size };
      }),
    setView: (view) => {
      cancelTween?.();
      cancelTween = null;
      set({ view });
    },
    fit: () => {
      const { image, viewport } = get();
      if (image && viewport.width > 0) {
        moveTo(fitView(image, viewport));
        set({ fitted: true });
      }
    },
    oneToOne: () => {
      const { image, viewport, view } = get();
      if (image) moveTo(oneToOneView(viewport, view));
    },
    zoomAt: (display, factor) => moveTo(zoomAround(get().view, display, factor)),
    zoomBy: (factor) => {
      const { viewport, view } = get();
      moveTo(zoomAround(view, { x: viewport.width / 2, y: viewport.height / 2 }, factor));
    },
    centreOn: (point, opts = {}) => {
      const { viewport, view } = get();
      if (viewport.width <= 0 || viewport.height <= 0) return;
      const scale =
        opts.radiusPx === undefined
          ? view.scale
          : clampScale(Math.min(viewport.width, viewport.height) / 3 / (2 * Math.max(1, opts.radiusPx)));
      moveTo({ scale, x: viewport.width / 2 - point.x * scale, y: viewport.height / 2 - point.y * scale }, opts.animate);
    },
    panIntoView: (rect, opts = {}) => {
      const s = get();
      if (s.viewport.width <= 0) return;
      const visible = viewportImageRect(s);
      const inside =
        rect.x >= visible.x &&
        rect.y >= visible.y &&
        rect.x + rect.w <= visible.x + visible.w &&
        rect.y + rect.h <= visible.y + visible.h;
      if (inside) return;
      const fits = Math.min((s.viewport.width * 0.8) / Math.max(1, rect.w), (s.viewport.height * 0.8) / Math.max(1, rect.h));
      const scale = clampScale(Math.min(s.view.scale, fits));
      const cx = rect.x + rect.w / 2;
      const cy = rect.y + rect.h / 2;
      moveTo({ scale, x: s.viewport.width / 2 - cx * scale, y: s.viewport.height / 2 - cy * scale }, opts.animate);
    },
    setKeepZoom: (on) => set({ keepZoom: on }),
    setInteracting: (on) => set((s) => (s.interacting === on ? s : { interacting: on })),
    setHeld: (keys) =>
      set((s) => ({
        spaceHeld: keys.space ?? s.spaceHeld,
        shiftHeld: keys.shift ?? s.shiftHeld,
        altHeld: keys.alt ?? s.altHeld,
      })),
    toggleAnnotations: () => set((s) => ({ showAnnotations: !s.showAnnotations })),
    toggleSuggestions: () => set((s) => ({ showSuggestions: !s.showSuggestions })),
    setThreshold: (value) => set({ threshold: Math.round(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, value)) * 100) / 100 }),
    beginRequest: () => set((s) => ({ pending: s.pending + 1 })),
    endRequest: () => set((s) => ({ pending: Math.max(0, s.pending - 1) })),
    fail: (message, retry) => set({ failure: { message, retry: retry ?? null } }),
    clearFailure: () => set({ failure: null }),
    openPicker: (at, purpose) => set({ picker: { at, purpose } }),
    closePicker: () => set({ picker: null }),
    setConfirm: (confirm) => set({ confirm }),
    linkFindings: (links) => set((s) => ({ findingOf: { ...s.findingOf, ...links } })),
    unlinkFinding: (boxId) =>
      set((s) => {
        if (!(boxId in s.findingOf)) return s;
        const findingOf = { ...s.findingOf };
        delete findingOf[boxId];
        return { findingOf };
      }),
    reset: () => {
      cancelTween?.();
      cancelTween = null;
      set({
        ...PER_IMAGE,
        imageId: null,
        image: null,
        boxes: {},
        order: [],
        measurements: {},
        view: INITIAL_VIEW,
        fitted: false,
        interacting: false,
        spaceHeld: false,
        shiftHeld: false,
        altHeld: false,
        history: new History(),
        pending: 0,
        failure: null,
      });
    },
  };
});

export type ImagesWorkspaceStore = UseBoundStore<StoreApi<ImagesWorkspaceState>>;
type S = ImagesWorkspaceState;

/** Accepted and edited shapes are ground truth; person shapes are created accepted. */
export function isAccepted(b: Box): boolean {
  return b.review_state === "accepted" || b.review_state === "edited";
}

/** Layer 2. Rejected and unreviewed shapes never appear here. */
export function acceptedShapes(s: Pick<S, "boxes" | "order" | "showAnnotations">): Box[] {
  if (!s.showAnnotations) return [];
  return s.order.map((id) => s.boxes[id]).filter((b): b is Box => !!b && isAccepted(b));
}

/** Layer 3's targets: unreviewed at or above the threshold, most confident first (spec §11.4). */
export function pendingSuggestions(s: Pick<S, "boxes" | "order" | "threshold" | "showSuggestions">): Box[] {
  if (!s.showSuggestions) return [];
  return s.order
    .map((id) => s.boxes[id])
    .filter((b): b is Box => !!b && b.review_state === "unreviewed" && (b.confidence ?? 1) >= s.threshold)
    .sort((a, b) => (b.confidence ?? 1) - (a.confidence ?? 1));
}

export function selectedShapes(s: Pick<S, "boxes" | "selectedIds">): Box[] {
  return s.selectedIds.map((id) => s.boxes[id]).filter((b): b is Box => !!b);
}

export function singleSelected(s: Pick<S, "boxes" | "selectedIds">): Box | null {
  return s.selectedIds.length === 1 ? (s.boxes[s.selectedIds[0]] ?? null) : null;
}

export function typeOf(s: Pick<S, "types">, typeId: string): ClassDef | undefined {
  return s.types.find((t) => t.id === typeId);
}

export function saveState(s: Pick<S, "pending" | "failure">): "saved" | "saving" | "failed" {
  if (s.failure) return "failed";
  return s.pending > 0 ? "saving" : "saved";
}

/** The part of the image on screen, in image px, clipped to the image (FA's SAM crop, §10). */
export function viewportImageRect(s: Pick<S, "view" | "viewport" | "image">): Rect {
  if (!s.image) return { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0 };
  const a = toImage({ x: 0, y: 0 }, s.view);
  const b = toImage({ x: s.viewport.width, y: s.viewport.height }, s.view);
  const x = Math.max(0, a.x);
  const y = Math.max(0, a.y);
  return {
    x,
    y,
    w: Math.max(0, Math.min(s.image.width, b.x) - x),
    h: Math.max(0, Math.min(s.image.height, b.y) - y),
  };
}

export function isDrawing(s: Pick<S, "draft">): boolean {
  return s.draft !== null;
}

export { ZOOM_STEP };

/** Resolves once no API call is in flight (auto-save before navigation). */
export function waitForIdle(store: ImagesWorkspaceStore = useImagesWorkspace, timeoutMs = 10_000): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (store.getState().pending === 0) return resolve();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      unsubscribe();
      resolve();
    }, timeoutMs);
    const unsubscribe = store.subscribe((s) => {
      if (s.pending === 0) {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        unsubscribe();
        resolve();
      }
    });
  });
}
```

Note: in `viewTween.ts`, `import.meta.env?.VITEST` is set by vitest; the timer path is what the
"animates" test drives with fake timers. If lint flags the optional chain on `import.meta.env`,
write `import.meta.env.VITEST`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the API calls**

Append to `frontend/src/api/shapes.ts` (and add `import type { ApiClient, Box, BoxCreate, BoxUpdate } from "@contract/client";`
and `import { unwrap } from "./errors";` at the top):

```ts
export function fetchImageDetail(api: ApiClient, projectId: string, imageId: string): Promise<ImageDetail> {
  return unwrap(
    api.GET("/api/v1/projects/{projectId}/images/{imageId}", { params: { path: { projectId, imageId } } }),
  ) as Promise<ImageDetail>;
}

export function createShape(api: ApiClient, projectId: string, imageId: string, body: BoxCreate): Promise<BoxWriteResult> {
  return unwrap(
    api.POST("/api/v1/projects/{projectId}/images/{imageId}/boxes", { params: { path: { projectId, imageId } }, body }),
  );
}

export function updateShape(
  api: ApiClient,
  projectId: string,
  boxId: string,
  body: BoxUpdate,
  opts: { confirmFindingDelete?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<BoxWriteResult> {
  return unwrap(
    api.PATCH("/api/v1/projects/{projectId}/boxes/{boxId}", {
      params: {
        path: { projectId, boxId },
        query: opts.confirmFindingDelete ? { confirm_finding_delete: true } : undefined,
      },
      body,
    }),
  );
}

export function reviewShapes(
  api: ApiClient,
  projectId: string,
  boxIds: string[],
  action: ShapeReviewAction,
): Promise<BoxReviewResult> {
  return unwrap(
    api.POST("/api/v1/projects/{projectId}/boxes/review", {
      params: { path: { projectId } },
      body: { box_ids: boxIds, action },
    }),
  );
}

export async function listImageMeasurements(
  api: ApiClient,
  projectId: string,
  imageId: string,
): Promise<ImageMeasurement[]> {
  const r = await unwrap(
    api.GET("/api/v1/projects/{projectId}/images/{imageId}/measurements", { params: { path: { projectId, imageId } } }),
  );
  return r.items;
}

export function createImageMeasurement(
  api: ApiClient,
  projectId: string,
  imageId: string,
  body: ImageMeasurementCreate,
): Promise<ImageMeasurement> {
  return unwrap(
    api.POST("/api/v1/projects/{projectId}/images/{imageId}/measurements", {
      params: { path: { projectId, imageId } },
      body,
    }),
  );
}

export async function deleteImageMeasurement(api: ApiClient, projectId: string, measurementId: string): Promise<void> {
  await unwrap<unknown>(
    api.DELETE("/api/v1/projects/{projectId}/image-measurements/{imageMeasurementId}", {
      params: { path: { projectId, imageMeasurementId: measurementId } },
    }),
  );
}

/** The create body that brings `box` back (undo of a delete, duplicate): only its shape's fields. */
export function bodyOf(box: Box): BoxCreate {
  if (box.shape === "polygon") {
    const body: BoxCreate = { class_id: box.class_id, shape: "polygon", points: box.points ?? [] };
    if (box.assist) body.assist = box.assist;
    return body;
  }
  if (box.shape === "point") return { class_id: box.class_id, shape: "point", x: box.x, y: box.y };
  return { class_id: box.class_id, shape: box.shape, x: box.x, y: box.y, w: box.w, h: box.h, angle: box.angle };
}
```

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { Box } from "@contract/client";
import { errorBody, fakeClient, IMAGE_ID, PROJECT_ID, type FakeRoute } from "@/test/fixtures";
import { useImagesWorkspace } from "@/store/imagesWorkspace";
import { makeDetail, makeMeasurement, makeShape, makeWritten } from "./testing";
import {
  cmdCreateMeasurement,
  cmdCreateShape,
  cmdDeleteMeasurement,
  cmdDeleteShapes,
  cmdRedo,
  cmdReview,
  cmdSetType,
  cmdUndo,
  cmdUpdateShape,
  type CommandContext,
} from "./commands";

let counter = 0;
const st = () => useImagesWorkspace.getState();

function ctxWith(extra: FakeRoute[] = []) {
  const routes: FakeRoute[] = [
    ...extra,
    {
      method: "POST",
      path: /\/images\/[^/]+\/boxes$/,
      status: 201,
      body: (req) => makeWritten({ ...(req.body as Partial<Box>), id: `new-${++counter}`, finding_id: "f-new" }),
    },
    {
      method: "PATCH",
      path: /\/boxes\/[^/]+$/,
      body: (req) => ({ ...st().boxes[req.url.split("/boxes/")[1].split("?")[0]], ...(req.body as object) }),
    },
    { method: "DELETE", path: /\/boxes\/[^/]+$/, status: 204, body: null },
    {
      method: "POST",
      path: /\/boxes\/review$/,
      body: { updated: 1, finding_ids_created: ["f9"], finding_ids_deleted: [] },
    },
    { method: "POST", path: /\/measurements$/, status: 201, body: makeMeasurement({ id: "m-new" }) },
    { method: "DELETE", path: /\/image-measurements\/[^/]+$/, status: 204, body: null },
  ];
  const { api, requests } = fakeClient(routes);
  const ctx: CommandContext = { api, projectId: PROJECT_ID, store: useImagesWorkspace, history: st().history };
  return { ctx, requests };
}

const polygon = makeShape({
  id: "p1",
  shape: "polygon",
  points: [[10, 10], [60, 10], [60, 50]],
  x: 10,
  y: 10,
  w: 50,
  h: 40,
});
const proposal = makeShape({
  id: "s1",
  review_state: "unreviewed",
  confidence: 0.8,
  provenance: { kind: "local_model", model_id: "m", provider: null, model_name: "M", query_run_id: null },
});

beforeEach(() => {
  st().reset();
  st().loadImage(makeDetail(), [polygon, proposal], [makeMeasurement({ id: "m1" })]);
});

describe("cmdCreateShape", () => {
  it("creates, selects, and undoes by deleting", async () => {
    const { ctx, requests } = ctxWith();
    const box = await cmdCreateShape(ctx, IMAGE_ID, { class_id: "c", shape: "polygon", points: [[0, 0], [9, 0], [0, 9]] });
    expect(box?.shape).toBe("polygon");
    expect(st().selectedIds).toEqual([box!.id]);
    expect(st().findingOf[box!.id]).toBe("f-new"); // FC-R16
    await cmdUndo(ctx);
    expect(st().boxes[box!.id]).toBeUndefined();
    expect(requests.at(-1)?.method).toBe("DELETE");
    await cmdRedo(ctx);
    expect(Object.values(st().boxes).some((b) => b.shape === "polygon" && b.id !== "p1")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("toasts a repaired polygon", async () => {
    const { ctx } = ctxWith([
      {
        method: "POST",
        path: /\/images\/[^/]+\/boxes$/,
        status: 201,
        body: makeWritten({ id: "rp", shape: "polygon", points: [[0, 0], [9, 0], [0, 9]], repaired: true }),
      },
    ]);
    const { useToastStore } = await import("@/ui/toastStore");
    await cmdCreateShape(ctx, IMAGE_ID, { class_id: "c", shape: "polygon", points: [[0, 0], [9, 0], [9, 9], [0, 9]] });
    expect(useToastStore.getState().toasts.at(-1)?.text).toMatch(/repaired/i);
  });

  it("records a failure with a retry that lands the shape", async () => {
    let fail = true;
    const { ctx } = ctxWith([
      {
        method: "POST",
        path: /\/images\/[^/]+\/boxes$/,
        status: () => (fail ? 500 : 201),
        body: () => {
          if (fail) {
            fail = false;
            return errorBody("internal", "boom");
          }
          return makeWritten({ id: "late" });
        },
      },
    ]);
    expect(await cmdCreateShape(ctx, IMAGE_ID, { class_id: "c", shape: "box", x: 1, y: 1, w: 9, h: 9 })).toBeUndefined();
    expect(st().failure?.message).toMatch(/draw shape failed/);
    st().failure!.retry!();
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    expect(st().failure).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("cmdUpdateShape", () => {
  it("patches polygon points and undoes to the old points", async () => {
    const { ctx, requests } = ctxWith();
    await cmdUpdateShape(ctx, "p1", { kind: "points", points: [{ x: 20, y: 20 }, { x: 70, y: 20 }, { x: 70, y: 60 }] });
    expect(requests.at(-1)?.body).toEqual({ points: [[20, 20], [70, 20], [70, 60]] });
    await cmdUndo(ctx);
    expect(requests.at(-1)?.body).toEqual({ points: [[10, 10], [60, 10], [60, 50]] });
  });

  it("does nothing when the geometry did not change", async () => {
    const { ctx, requests } = ctxWith();
    await cmdUpdateShape(ctx, "p1", { kind: "points", points: [{ x: 10, y: 10 }, { x: 60, y: 10 }, { x: 60, y: 50 }] });
    expect(requests).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

describe("cmdSetType", () => {
  it("asks for confirmation on finding_would_be_deleted and retries with confirm", async () => {
    const { ctx, requests } = ctxWith([
      {
        method: "PATCH",
        path: /\/boxes\/p1$/,
        status: (req) => (req.url.includes("confirm_finding_delete=true") ? 200 : 409),
        body: (req) =>
          req.url.includes("confirm_finding_delete=true")
            ? { ...polygon, class_id: "object" }
            : errorBody("finding_would_be_deleted", "would delete F-0001"),
      },
    ]);
    expect(await cmdSetType(ctx, ["p1"], "object")).toBe("needs-confirm");
    expect(st().boxes.p1.class_id).toBe(polygon.class_id);
    expect(await cmdSetType(ctx, ["p1"], "object", { confirmFindingDelete: true })).toBe("done");
    expect(requests.at(-1)?.url).toContain("confirm_finding_delete=true");
  });
});

describe("cmdReview", () => {
  it("accepts, returns the created finding ids, and undoes with unreview", async () => {
    const { ctx, requests } = ctxWith();
    const r = await cmdReview(ctx, ["s1"], "accept");
    expect(r?.finding_ids_created).toEqual(["f9"]);
    expect(st().boxes.s1.review_state).toBe("accepted");
    await cmdUndo(ctx);
    expect((requests.at(-1)?.body as { action: string }).action).toBe("unreview");
    expect(st().boxes.s1.review_state).toBe("unreviewed");
  });

  it("undo of an accept refused with finding_has_content keeps the entry", async () => {
    const { ctx } = ctxWith();
    await cmdReview(ctx, ["s1"], "accept");
    const refusing = ctxWith([
      {
        method: "POST",
        path: /\/boxes\/review$/,
        status: 409,
        body: errorBody("finding_has_content", "has a note"),
      },
    ]).ctx;
    await cmdUndo(refusing);
    expect(st().boxes.s1.review_state).toBe("accepted");
    expect(st().history.canUndo()).toBe(true);
    expect(st().failure?.message).toBe("This finding has a note or photos; delete it from the inspector.");
  });
});

describe("cmdDeleteShapes", () => {
  it("deletes person shapes, rejects proposals, and undo re-creates the polygon with its points", async () => {
    const { ctx, requests } = ctxWith();
    await cmdDeleteShapes(ctx, ["p1", "s1"]);
    expect(st().boxes.p1).toBeUndefined();
    expect(st().boxes.s1.review_state).toBe("rejected");
    await cmdUndo(ctx);
    const create = requests.find((r) => r.method === "POST" && /\/boxes$/.test(r.url));
    expect(create?.body).toMatchObject({ shape: "polygon", points: [[10, 10], [60, 10], [60, 50]] });
    expect(st().boxes.s1.review_state).toBe("unreviewed");
  });
});

describe("measurements", () => {
  it("creates and deletes with undo", async () => {
    const { ctx } = ctxWith();
    await cmdCreateMeasurement(ctx, IMAGE_ID, { x: 0, y: 0 }, { x: 30, y: 40 });
    expect(st().measurements["m-new"]).toBeDefined();
    await cmdDeleteMeasurement(ctx, "m1");
    expect(st().measurements.m1).toBeUndefined();
    await cmdUndo(ctx);
    expect(Object.keys(st().measurements)).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});
